TANG SHIKYNTIEN KWAI 


aci ka Poitrl ? Kaei ka ’mynslein’ Jong ka ne kaei ka 

P i^dor Jong ka ? Kl long kl jingkylll kiba u Bah Kynpham 
Sing Nongkynrih, kum u nongthoh poltri bad kum u 
nonghikai litereshor, u shah kylli bad nangta p.vrshang 
ban jubab lyngba kane ka jingpyrshang kaba n ai kyrteng 
Ban Sngewthuh ia ka Poitri. 


Kum ban shu kynthoh tiak, ka jingpyrshang ban batal 
shaphang ka poltxi da ki nongthoh Khasi ba pher ba phcr 
kam del kaba dang mynhynnin wat lada kita ki jingthoh 
kim da long kiba pura ha ka dur ban thew ia ka poltri da 
ki ‘diengkot’ ba bun jait. Ka jingpule ia ka sobjek ba ki 
khot ka 'Literary theory and Criticism’ kaba kynthup rnh 
hapoh ka jylli Jong ka ia ka jingblshar bniah ia ka poitri. 
ka la sdang naduh ba la pile ia ka Khasi Honours ha 
Kolej, kata, naduh kl snem Jong u hynniewphew. Hapoh 
kane ka tnat-pule ka litereshor, ngi lap ia ki nongthoh 
kiba la pyrshang ban ‘batai’ kaei ka Poitri — kum u 
Edrenel Chyne, Soso Tham, Hopewell Elias, Primrose 
Gatphoh bad kham tarn lei lei u Frank Marpna Pugh uba 
la pyrshang ban lamphang shaphang kaei ka relorik bad 
ka prosodi.

Bun na kitel kl nongthoh Jong ngi kiba long ruh kum 
kl ‘literary critics’ kim da kren pynshai than shaphang 
’kaei ka poitri’ wat lada kl la pyrshang ban ktah mationg 
la ka ‘mynslem’ Jong ka. 

Kam pher bad niuma, ban pyrshang kham bha bad 
ban dang pyndap shuh shuh la kaba duna halor ka 
jingbatai shaphang ka poltri wan mih paw mynta u Bah 
Kynpham Nongkynrih — u brlew uba la shah phyrngop 
ha kl dew ka poitrl-ym kum uba la shah pynngam jyllop 
— hynrel uba la suhkhlleh sha kl khuifi Jong ka, kjit la 
ka mynslem Jong ka bad nangta mih blult da kaba pyrsad 
mynslem ban lathuh kaei ka lyntl de nc kumno ban 
sngewthuh kham shal kham bha kaei ka poitri. 



Ban Sngewthuh ia ka Poitri 


7 


Ha kane ka khep banyngkong tam lehse, U Bah 
Kynpham u pynpaw pyrthei ia lade ym kum u myllung 
(poet) hynrel kum u nongthir nongthalh shapheing ki jnit 
ki Jnat kiba ia snoh bad ka poitri, lane kaba ngi leih ban 
ong ruh shaphang kumno ba ka poitri kum ka tyllong um 
ba pun bad mih na ka longbrlew hi bad kylla sha ka wah 
bah bad duriaw ka dohnud brlew ka don la ki riam ki 
beit bad ki khor ki khriam khnang ba kan Im khrek ha 
khmat ka pyrthei. Lada nga tam ktien ban ong el ei shuh, 
shah ia nga ban ong kumne namar nga sngew ba ka 
poitri ka long:

“Ki khutn ba shong syrtap ha ka dohnud bad Jabieng briew, 
ha ka Jinglong lui lui bad synlar, ba pynpaw ia Jingshisha 
ka Jingsngew, ba pun ba khih wit, lyngba ki Jingshem 
jingiakynduh, mih bluil shabar lyngba ka ktien ba 
pyniahap tar bad kita baroh. ” 

U Bah Kynpham kum u briew u ban iapnoh ia ka 
poitri. lehse, un ym hun tang kaei kaba nga ong namar u 
angnud ba ki nongthoh poitri (khamtam eh) bad ki 
nongpule poitri hapdeng ka jaitbynriew jong ngi kin 
sngewthuh kin shemphang bad hiewkor — kaei ka Poitri ! 

Ngam bakla ban ong ba del halor kata ka Jlngangnud 
ba hir hir ba U Bah Kynpham u pynmih ia kane ka kot 
kaba u nongthoh, nga ngeit skhem, u la theh lut bad 
por bad bor, ym tang na ka ‘por’ ba u tuh na lade hynrel 
na ka ‘bor’ ba dawa tyngeh la ka jingshemphang jong u, 
bad kumno kane kan iarap ia kito kiba ieid la ka poitri 
lada ha ka pule, ka thoh bad hikai ia ka ha kane ka Ri 
jong ngi. 


Bevan L. Swer 

Professor & Head 
Department of Khasi 
NEHU

bad dei la kane ba i Bah Kynpham 1 la pyrshang ba'n

ki la noh s)mhiang shibun kiei-kiei klba kordor ha ka mat

SHAPHANG U NONGTHOH 


Kynpham Sing Nongkynrih u dei u Lecturer Jong 
II ka English ha Sankardev College bad tat haduh u 
snem 1996, u dei ruh u Editor banyngkong Jong ka 
Apphira Daily News, kaba la pynmih ha u snem 1994. La 
kha ( 1 964) bad pynheh pynsan ia u ha Sohra. U pyndep ia 
ka High School na Government Boys’, Shillong, ia ka B.A. 
na St. Anthony’s Collegf^ bad la ka M.A. (English) na 
NEllU. Myntu u dang leh ‘research’ na ka hynta ka PhD ha 
ka English. 

U nongthoh u dei ruh u my Hung ba la ithuh da kiba 
bun bad ha ka snem 1995 la khot sngewbha ia u ban leit 
iur ia ka Wales herd England kum shihyntajong kajingrakhe 
ta ka “U.K. Year of Lilcrature and Writing, 1995". Ia ki 
potm Jong u ha ka Pharertg la pynmih ha kiba bun ki 
Journal’ bad ki 'anihology' kum ha ka “Indian Literature" 
(Sahitya Akadcmi, New Delhi); ""rhe Journal" (Poetry Society, 
India, New DelhQ; “IMerut lire Alive" (British Council Division, 
New Delhi): “Poiesis" (Bombay): “Femina" (Bombay): “'I'he 
Telegraph" (Calcutta): “The New Welsh Review'’ (Cardiff, 
Wales); “Green's Mugazuie” (Sasketchewan, Caiiada); “Spin” 
(Auckland, New Tk’aland): “World Poetry 1993" (Madras): 
“The American Poetry Anthology" (Santa Cruz, U.S.A) bad 
ha ka “Khasia in Gwalia" (Swansea, Wales). Ha ka snem 
1992, ka Writers Workshop, Calcutta ka la pynmih ia or 
iylli ki kot poitri Jong u, kynihup ia ka Moments bad The 
Sieve. Ka thup poitri balai Jong u kan sa mih shen.

U nongthoh u dei ruh u ‘translator’ bad ‘essayist’ ha 
ka Khasi bad Phareng. Ia ka thup ki khanatang Khasi, 
ba u la pynkylla sha ka Phareng, yn sa pynmih shen da 
ka Alwi Books, Port Talbot. Wales. Ha ka por ba u sdang 
ia ka Apphira, u la pynmih ia ka Handbook for Apphira

KAEI KA POITRI ? 


umba la ong haneng. ka long da shisha kaba J 3 mjar 
ijlji f^ban batai shaphang kaei ka poitri. Ka babha tarn 
11!^^ kaba ngi lah ban leh hangne ka long belt tang ban 
kdew ia ki mat bakongsan jong ka poitri bashlsha bad 
ban ai jlngbated katto katne shaphang jong ki. Hynrel 
shuwa ba ngin poi shata, to ngin ia weng noh shuwa ia ki 
jiiTgpynthame baroh kiba don hapdeng ka poitri bad 
ka Verse. 


la ka verse ngim lah ban ong ba ka dei ka poitri wat 
la bun ki myllung ki ju thoh poitri ha ka dur Jong ka. Ka 
verse ka dei tang ka shynrong ne ka dur met jong ka 
poitri. bad don bun ki jingthoh ba la thoh ha ka dur jong 
ka ki byrn don dor poitri ei ei. Kumba la ong hanengkba 
verse ka dei tang ka met bad ka poitri ka dei ka mynslem 
kaba ai jingim ia ka met. 

1 lynrei kumno yn lah ban pyniapher ia ka verse na ka 
Prose, ne ka rukom thoh beit ia ki ktien kumba kren ? 


la ka verse la ju thoh ha ka rukom kaba kham pher 
bad bunsicn la ju phiah ia ka sha ki katto katne dkhot, 
kiba ngi khot ki Stanzas (Stansa), kiba long kumjuh 
thik ha ka dur, Kum ban shu batai khyndiat, lada ka

Narrative Poetry (Ka Naretip Poitri) 

la u nongthoh Naretip Poitri la Ju ong ba u dei u

Kl Jait Mltar 

Ka mltar ka del ka kynja dur kaba pynpaw kumno la 
thew ia kl lyngkhot sur saw (sound — units) kiba pynlong 
ia ka ridiin bad kiba lai wan paw ha kajuh ka dur ha ki 
lain jong kano ma ka polm. Ka dur jong ka mltar pat ka 
shong eh ha ka rukom p)mbeit la kl stansa; ha ka Jlngbun 
ki phut (feet) ha ka shl lain; bad ruh ha kl jait phut 
bapher ba la pyndonkam ha ka poim. Ka Jingpule ia ka 
mitar namarkata ka dei ruh ka Jingbatai ia kata ka phut 
(foot) ne ka Jingthew ia ki lain poitri da ki silabil (syllables) 
ne ki sur kynnoh kiba don ha ka shikyntien, namar ia ka 
phut hi la pynlong da katto katne ki silabil bakhia 
(stressed) bad basting (unstressecfi bad la buh lang ha ka 
shi lain. Kumban shu pyni nuksa, ka aiambik pentamitar 
ka dei ka Jait mitar kaba don san phut ne shiphew silabil 
ha ka shi lain, kaba sdang da ka silabil ka basting bud sa 
da ka silabil ka bakhia. Lada ngin pule ia kine ki sentens 
hcirum ngin sa sngewthuh kham shai kiei kita ki silabil 
kiba pynlong ia ka phut kl del (kl 'stroke’ hangne kl pyni 
la ki silabil kiba khia) :

Ki Jait Mitar 


Dei da kum kane ka jingthew lain namarkata ba ka 
mitar ka iarap ban wanrah ia ka rldim baryntlh ha ka 
poitrl bad pynlapher la ka na ka ridim bym ryntih jong 
ka prose. 

la ka verse lah ban pynbit pynbiang ha kawei na kine 
ki saw Jait kl mitar bakongsan : 

(i) Ka Quantitative Metre (Kwantitetiv Mitar) ia kiiba 
la ju p)Tidonkam ha ki jingthoh Greek bad Latin. Kane ka 
jait mitar ka k3mthup ia ka jingpynbcit marbud ia ki silabil 
bajrong bad balyngkot (long and short syllables) ha ki 
shlkynhun kynhun bala khot ki phut. 

(ii) Ka Syllabic Metre (Silabik Mitar) ia kaba la ju 
pyndonkam ha ki jingthoh jong ki F'rench bad Japanese. 
Kane ka mitar ka don katta tylli (fixed number) ki silabil 
kumba la mang khnang na ka bynia shi lain. 

(iii) Accentual Metre (Ka aksenshwal Mitar) ia kaba 
la ju pyndonkam ha ki jingthoh barim jong ki Pharcng. 
Kane ka mitar ka long kaba khia thew bha bad ka kynthup 
ia katto katne ki silabil bakhia ba la mang khnang na ka 
bynta shi lain, khlem da pynsalia pat la ki silabil basting 
ki tarn ne dune.

(iv) Ka Accentual - Syllabic Metre (Aksenshwal - 
Silabik Mitar) kaba kynthup ia katto katne ki silabil 
bakhia ba la pynbeit kyrpang lang bad ki silabil basting 
ha ka shi lain. Tangba hangne u myllung u niew ym 
tang ia ki silabil bakhia hynrei ruh ia ki basting khnang 
ba un ioh ia katta tylli kl silabil kumba la mang na ka 
bynta shi lain. 

Kane ka jait mitar ka basaw ka del kaba ju pyndonkam 
eh da ki nongthoh poitrl Phareng naduh ka por jong u 
Chaucer shaneng. Lah pat ban pyniabynta shuh shuh ia 
kane ka mitar sha kl ar Iqmhun katkum ka rukom p5mbelt 
ia ki silabil bakhia bad basting ha ka shi lain. Kine ki ar 
kynhun kidei ka Duple Metre (Oiwpil Mitar) bad ka 
Triple Metre (Tripil Mitar).

(Critical Terms)

nikorn spel Pharr ng.

Abstract (Abstrak) : Kane ka kti 'n ka dei kaba batai 
sliaphang kano kano ka poini kaba kren belt tang ia ki

na ka poim “Essay on Man** jong u Alexander Pope ki long

**Hope springs eternal in the human breast, 

Man never is, but always to be, blest. **

Amblquity (Ambikwiti) : Kane ka dei ka jlnglah Jong 
kano kano ka kyntlen ban kit ar ne lai jingmut ha kajuh 
ka por. Dei na kata ka daw ba ki sa ong ba ka ambikwiti

kyntlen da u William Empson ha ka Seven Types of

ka dei na ka poim u Miklos Radnoti ka “Seventh Eclogue”.

baroh. Ilynrel wat la katta ruh. u myllung um lah ban 
pdiang ia ka Jlngwan Jong ka namar ka Jlngthrang eh Jong 
u ban ioh sa shisien ia la ka baieit.

Anaphora : Kane ka del ka jingial-ong biang ia ka 
ktlen ne ka kynhun ki kyntien (phrase) ha kaba sdang 
jong kl katto katne ki lain jong kano kano ka poim. Kine

Happy Letter !“ kin pynshai kham bha shaphang ka 
anaphora : 

“Going to him ! Happy letter ! 

Tell Him — 

Tell Him the page I didn't write — 

Tell Him — I only said the syntax — ”

Da ap, da ap, ko ieiV ! 

U sangeh khek, - shahe u rkhie ! 

Ka peit lieb, — da u Bieit 1"

Apostrophe (Apostrophi) : Kane ka del ka bult thoh

Archetjrpe (Arkitaip) : Kane ka del ka ktlen ka balar 
bad ka mut ka kynja dur (symbol), ka phangpdeng (theme) 
ne u tyngshop (character) la kiba kl nongthoh kl ju lal 
pyndonkam ha ki kot Jong kl. La ju iai pyndonkam la 
kum kine kiei kiei namar ka jingdon jong ki la katto

phangpdeng ki long kum ka jingieit, ka jlnglap bad kl 
kynja thma etc.; bad ki tyngshop pat ki long kum kl hiro 
ka por ba mynshuwa ia kiba la batal ha kane ka L)mnong

kaba iadei bad ki anti-hiro. Ha ki poitri Ph£U"eng kl don 
bun kiba kum kine ki hiro bad uba kham paw u del u 
“Don Juan” ba la thoh da u Lord Byron. 

Ha ka Khasi ka nuksa jong kum kine ki tyngshop lah 
ban ong bad ka del ka “Nam” ha ka polm u Soso Tham. 

Assonance (Asonans) : Kane ka del ka jingiaiong biang 
ia kl sur kynnoh jong kl vowel (vowel sounds) ha kl 
kyntlen ba labud kum ha kine kl nuksa : 

“Sweet dreams”; “hit or miss” 

Ha kltel kl nuksa ngl iohi ba kl sur kynnoh Jong ki 
vowel kl la long kumjuh, kata ha ka nuksa banyngkong.

ngi loh ia ki sur “swee" bad “drea”: katba ha ka nuksa

Augustan (Akostan) ; Kane ka ktien ka kdew sha ki 
nongthoh, ki jingihoh ne kiei kiei kiba iadon bynla bad

litereshor jong ki Roman, ha kaba la im ki nongthoh

La ioh ia ka kyrteng jong kane ka juk na ka kyrteng u

Ha ka “E^iglish literature”, ka ktien Akostan ka kdew 
ia ka por jong u John Dryden, u Alexander Pope bad u 
Jonathan Swift; bad kata ka mut hapdeng ki snem 1670 
bad 1780. la ka juk kitei ki nongthoh la khot ka juk 
Akostan namar ba ki ia bud ia ki rukom thoh poitri jong 
kitei ki nongthoh Roman haneng, khamtam lei ia ka rukom 
thoh poitri episil bad sataiar.

tbian.

Kane ka ktien ka wan na ka ktien Greek kaba mut

Catharsis (Katharsis): Kane ka ladon bynta bad ka

Lynnong'll ha bakut).

niflcaioai (Klasikal): Klasikal ka dei ka ktien kaba 
kdew sha ki Jingthoh Jong ki Greek bad ki Roman bad 
ruh sha ki Jingthoh ki babud ia ka rukom thoh Jong ki 
nongthoh Greek bad Roman (peit ruh “Akostan ). la ka 
mynta hi kane ka ktien ka batai shaphang kito ki nongthoh 
kiba kham ban ia ka rukom thoh ka barynlih, ka bym 
noh sMUmg tang sha ki jingsngew. ka batylli mlon bad ka 
bym pynpaw than eh tang ia kajinglong bad Jingsngew u 
nongthoh. U "classical writer” namarkata u dei u nongthoh 
uba lah bha ban teh lakam (restrain) la la kl Jong ki 
Jingsngew, uba lah ruh ban buhrieh ia ka Jinglong la Jong 
(individualism) bad uba bud pyrkhing bha la kl ain. ki

“As when some hwiter in the spring hath found 
A breeding eagle silting on her nesL 
UjiKni the craggy isle of a hill-lake, 

And pierced her with an arrow as she rose. 

And follow'd her to find where she fell 
Far ojf, - 'anon her mate comes winging back 
From hunting, and a great way off decries 
His huddlu^g young left sole : at that, he checks 
His pinion, and with short uneasy sweeps 
Circles above his eyry. with loud screams 
Chiding his mate back to her nest ; but she 
Lie: dying, with the arrow in her side. 

In some far stony gorge out of his ken, 

A heap of fluttering feather — never more 
Shall the lake glass her. flying over it; 

Never the black and dripping precipices 
Echo her stormy screams as she sails by — 

As that poor bird flies home, nor knows his loss,

So Ruslum knew nut his own loss, but stood 
Over his dying son, and knew him not. ”

la kum kata ka sobjek. Kane ka lapher na ka vnok epik 
bad ka berlesk namar, katba kine ki thoh shaphang ka 
sobjek kamkai ha ka rukom ba 1 shongkun bha, ma ka 
pat ka pynman kamkai ia ka sobjek bashongkhia katno 
katno ruh. 

Trimetre (Traimitar): Kane ka dei ka lain poitri baled 
phut, kynthup la lai tylli ki silabil bakhia (peit ruh 
Lynnong-IV, “Jail Mitaf.

Triple Rhyme (Tripil Raim): Kane ka del ka raim lai 
wat ne ka raim kaba iahap sur hapdeng lai tylli ki kynnoh 
ne silabil, kum ha ki kyntien “beauiiful - du<i/Ul” ne 
“basharai - bashalaC. 

Triplet: Kane kum ka tersel ka lah ban long ka stansa 
balal lain ne ka bynta balai lain jong kano kano ka stansa 
ba la thoh ha ki hiroik kuplet. Kum ki hiroik kuplet, ki 
lain triplet ruh ki dei ban don ia ka juh ka raim. Kine ki 
lain harum na ka poim u Pope, ka “Essay on Criticism’’ 
kin ieng kum ka nuksa : 

“Music resembles poetry, in each 

Are nameless Graces which no methods teach. 

And which a Master-Hand alone can reach.” 

Trochee (Trokhi): Kane ka dei ka jalt phut kaba 
kynthup ia ar tylli ki silabil, sdang da ka bakhia, bud sa 
da ka basting (peit mh Lynnong-lV, “Jail Mitaf). 

Troubadour (Trubador); Une u dei u myllung uba Im 
ha ka bynta bashathie jong ka France ne ka bynta bashatei 
jong ka Itall ha kata ka juk Middle Age. Ym kum u 
minstrel (peit “MinstreV ha kane ka Lynnong) u trubador 
u del u myllung na ka iing bakhraw batri, uba thoh ia ki 
poitri jingieit ba bun jnit bun jnat. 

Utopia (lutopia): Kane ka dei ka pyrthei na ka Imlang 
ka sahlang (society) ba la shu mutdur da u nongthoh.

Verbose (Verbose): Kane ka dei ka rukom thoh kaba 
bun ktlen eh jaktak bad kaba pynsniew dur pynban ia ka

Weak Ending (Wiik Ending): Kc. e ka del ka jingpynkut 
lain ka batlot, ha kaba la ka silabil basting la buh l)a ka 
jaka jong ka silabil bakhia. Kane harum ka dei ka nuksa

“Thy mother was a piece of virtue, and 

She said thou wasi my daughter.” 

Kane harum pat ka dei ka nuksa na ka blank verse 
jong u H. W, Sten ha Ka Burom ba la Jah: 

“U Syiem Jong ngi u blei Jong ngi, phi wan 

Piengksiar niamtra, ” 

Ngi del ban kynmaw ruh ba ha ka wiik ending yin ju 
don uno uno u dak sangeh ha bakut jong u lain, kumba 
la pyni ha kite! kl nuksa.

(i) “Season of mists and mellow Jhiiifulness, 

Close bosom friend of the maturing sun : 

Conspiring with him how to load and bless 
With fruit the vines that round the thatch-eaves run; 
To bend with apples the moss’d cottage-trees. 

And fUl all fruit with ripeness to the core; 

To swell the gourd, and plump the hazel shells 
With a sweet kernel; to set budding more 
And still more, later flower for the bees. 

Until they think warm days will never cease; 

For summer has o'erbrunm'd their clammy cells”.
